0001  000000  %#MODEL nd100
0002  000000  %
0003  000000  % ND-100 MICROCODE, 32 BIT FLOATING POINT VARIANT (PROM SET 026XX), ON THE 3033S BASE
0004  000000  %
0005  000000  % This file is the 3033S program (ND-100-3033S.uc) with the words 0517 - 1105 replaced by the
0006  000000  % printed listing "32-BITS ND-100 FLOATING (PROM 026XX)" (printout of 16.12.1985; scan
0007  000000  % ND100-MICROCODE.pdf, pages 221-233). There is NO chip dump of the 026XX PROMs, so the
0008  000000  % instruction words of the replaced part cannot be compared with a ROM image. What was checked
0009  000000  % is that the file assembles, that every label resolves, and that the 1801 words outside 0517 -
0010  000000  % 1105 are bit for bit the words of the 3033S chips (so the entry points FSB, FAD, FMU, FDV, NLZ,
0011  000000  % DNZ and IOX of the 32 bit routines are at the same addresses as in the 48 bit routines).
0012  000000  % CYCL: as in ND-100-3033S.uc, a printed CYCL,FAST or missing CYCL is written CYCL,SLOW.
0013  000000  %
0014  000000  %********************************************************************
0015  000000  % 48-BIT N-VERSION
0016  000000  %********************************************************************
0017  000000  % ADDRESS 0 ENTERED AT :
0018  000000  % MASTER CLEAR
0019  000000  % PAGE FAULT
0020  000000  % PROTECT VIOLATION
0021  000000  % ROM OUT OF RANGE
0022  000000  %********************************************************************
0023  000000  0/
0024  000000            A,17 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
0025  000000            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0026  000000            COND,FETCH F,NEXT F,HOLD;
0027  000001            A,15 ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,Q
0028  000001            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0029  000001            FTCH CONDENABL;
0030  000002  %********************************************************************
0031  000002  % INCREMENT P IF NOT FETCH
0032  000002            B,P ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
0033  000002            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
0034  000003  FTCH:     A,13 ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,Q
0035  000003            IDBS,BMG COMM,CLIRQ T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0036  000003            COND,IRQ F,JMP F,HOLD;
0037  000004            ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
0038  000004            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0039  000004            NOINT CONDENABL;
0040  000005  %********************************************************************
0041  000005  % INTERRUPT IS PENDING
0042  000005            ALUD,NONE
0043  000005            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0044  000005            COND,STP F,JMP F,HOLD;
0045  000006            ALUD,NONE
0046  000006            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0047  000006            FTCH2 CONDENABL;
0048  000007  %********************************************************************
0049  000007  % CPU IS IN STOP MODE, MULTIPLE SINGLE OR BREAKPOINT MUST BE THE CASE
0050  000007            AB,BPFLG ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE
0051  000007            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0052  000007            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
0053  000010            AB,SINGL ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE
0054  000010            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0055  000010            BRKZZ CONDENABL;
0056  000011            ALUD,NONE
0057  000011            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0058  000011            BRKZZ CONDENABL;
0059  000012  %********************************************************************
0060  000012  % NO INTERRUPT IS PENDING, IT CAN BE MASTER CLEAR OR IOF-PAGEFAULT
0061  000012  NOINT:    ALUD,NONE
0062  000012            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0063  000012            COND,FETCH F,NEXT F,HOLD;
0064  000013            ALUD,NONE
0065  000013            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0066  000013            FTCH2 CONDENABL;
0067  000014  %********************************************************************
0068  000014  % DECREMENT P, IT HAS BEEN INCREMENTED
0069  000014            B,P ALUF,B-1 ALUD,B
0070  000014            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0071  000014            FTCH2;
0072  000015  %********************************************************************
0073  000015  % EXECUTE LEVEL CHANGE OR JMP *
0074  000015  FTCH2:    PIC,RVECT ALUD,NONE
0075  000015            IDBS,GPR T,MAPJ T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0076  000015            FETCH;
0077  000016  %********************************************************************
0078  000016  %********************************************************************
0079  000016  % MAPPING POINT FOR ALL INSTRUCTIONS. NEXT MICROADDRESS IS TAKEN FROM
0080  000016  % MAPPING PROM, OR CONTROLLED BY THE INTERRUPT HARDWARE (INTERRUPT VECTOR)
0081  000016  %********************************************************************
0082  000016  FETCH:    A,P B,Z ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B,YA
0083  000016            IDBS,ALU MIS,FETCH COMM,RDRQ T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW;
0084  000017  %********************************************************************
0085  000017  % RETURN FROM P-MODIFYING ROP- OR BOP-INSTRUCTION
0086  000017  PMODX:    ALUD,NONE
0087  000017            IDBS,DSABL T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
0088  000020  %********************************************************************
0089  000020  % RETURN AFTER JUMPS, OR WHENEVER NEW FETCH OF INSTRUCTION HAS TO BE DONE
0090  000020  INIT:     B,P ALUF,PASSB ALUD,NONE
0091  000020            IDBS,ALU MIS,FETCH COMM,RDRQ T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW;
0092  000021  %********************************************************************
0093  000021  % RETURN WITH NEXT INSTRUCTION IN DBR
0094  000021  USDBR:    PIC,RVECT B,P ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
0095  000021            IDBS,DBR COMM,LDGPR T,MAPJ T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0096  000021            FETCH;
0097  000022  %********************************************************************
0098  000022  % RETURN AFTER STORE-INSTRUCTIONS, WITH NEXT INSTRUCTION IN GPR
0099  000022  STEND:    A,P B,R1 ALUF,A-B ALUD,NONE
0100  000022            IDBS,ALU COMM,ESRP T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0101  000022            COND,R=P F,JMP F,HOLD;
0102  000023            PIC,RVECT B,P ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
0103  000023            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,MAPJ T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0104  000023            FETCH CONDENABL;
0105  000024  %********************************************************************
0106  000024  % *+1 HAS BEEN CHANGED
0107  000024            PIC,LOSTS B,P ALUF,B-1 ALUD,B
0108  000024            IDBS,AARG COMM,EPIC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0109  000024            10;
0110  000025            ALUD,NONE
0111  000025            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0112  000025            INIT;
0113  000026  %********************************************************************
0114  000026  % RETURN WITH NEXT INSTRUCTION IN GPR
0115  000026  USGPR:    PIC,RVECT B,P ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
0116  000026            IDBS,GPR T,MAPJ T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0117  000026            FETCH;
0118  000027  %********************************************************************
0119  000027  % CODE FOR ILLEGAL INTRUCTION
0120  000027  ILLIN:    A,7 ALUD,NONE
0121  000027            IDBS,BMG COMM,SMPID T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0122  000027            INIT;
0123  000030  %********************************************************************
0124  000030  % CODE FOR PRIVILEGED INSTRUCTION IF ON RING 0-1
0125  000030  PRIVI:    A,11 ALUD,NONE
0126  000030            IDBS,BMG COMM,SMPID T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0127  000030            INIT;
0128  000031  %********************************************************************
0129  000031  %********************************************************************
0130  000031  %
0131  000031  % STORE INSTRUCTIONS
0132  000031  %
0133  000031  %********************************************************************
0134  000031  STZ:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0135  000031            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0136  000031            MREFS;
0137  000032            ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
0138  000032            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0139  000032            STEND;
0140  000033  STA:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0141  000033            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0142  000033            MREFS;
0143  000034            A,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0144  000034            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0145  000034            STEND;
0146  000035  STT:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0147  000035            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0148  000035            MREFS;
0149  000036            A,T ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0150  000036            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0151  000036            STEND;
0152  000037  STX:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0153  000037            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0154  000037            MREFS;
0155  000040            A,X ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0156  000040            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0157  000040            STEND;
0158  000041  STD:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0159  000041            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0160  000041            MREFS;
0161  000042            A,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0162  000042            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
0163  000042            WRRQ;
0164  000043            A,D ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0165  000043            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0166  000043            STEND;
0167  000044  STF:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0168  000044            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0169  000044            MREFS;
0170  000045            A,T ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0171  000045            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
0172  000045            WRRQ;
0173  000046            A,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0174  000046            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
0175  000046            WRRQ;
0176  000047            A,D ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0177  000047            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0178  000047            STEND;
0179  000050  MIN:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0180  000050            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0181  000050            MREFL;
0182  000051            B,R2 ALUF,D+1 ALUD,B
0183  000051            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0184  000051            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
0185  000052            B,R1 ALUF,PASSB ALUD,NONE
0186  000052            IDBS,ALU COMM,WRRQ MIS,HOLD T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0187  000052            COND,COND F,JMP F,POP;
0188  000053            A,R2 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0189  000053            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0190  000053            STEND CONDENABL;
0191  000054            B,P ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
0192  000054            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0193  000054            INIT;
0194  000055  %********************************************************************
0195  000055  %********************************************************************
0196  000055  % LOAD INSTRUCTIONS
0197  000055  %
0198  000055  %********************************************************************
0199  000055  LDA:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0200  000055            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0201  000055            MREFL;
0202  000056            B,A ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
0203  000056            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0204  000056            USGPR;
0205  000057  LDT:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0206  000057            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0207  000057            MREFL;
0208  000060            B,T ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
0209  000060            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0210  000060            USGPR;
0211  000061  LDX:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0212  000061            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0213  000061            MREFL;
0214  000062            B,X ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
0215  000062            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0216  000062            USGPR;
0217  000063  LDD:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0218  000063            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0219  000063            MREFL;
0220  000064  LDAD:     B,A ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
0221  000064            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
0222  000064            RDRQ;
0223  000065            B,D ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
0224  000065            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0225  000065            USGPR;
0226  000066  LDF:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0227  000066            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0228  000066            MREFL;
0229  000067            B,T ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
0230  000067            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
0231  000070  NEXT:     B,R1 ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
0232  000070            IDBS,ALU MIS,HOLD COMM,RDRQ T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0233  000070            LDAD;
0234  000071  ADD:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0235  000071            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0236  000071            MREFL;
0237  000072            A,A B,A ALUF,D+A ALUD,B STS,EA
0238  000072            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0239  000072            USGPR;
0240  000073  SUB:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0241  000073            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0242  000073            MREFL;
0243  000074            A,A B,A ALUF,A-D ALUD,B STS,EA
0244  000074            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0245  000074            USGPR;
0246  000075  AND:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0247  000075            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0248  000075            MREFL;
0249  000076            A,A B,A ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,B
0250  000076            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0251  000076            USGPR;
0252  000077  ORA:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0253  000077            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0254  000077            MREFL;
0255  000100            A,A B,A ALUF,ORDA ALUD,B
0256  000100            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0257  000100            USGPR;
0258  000101  %********************************************************************
0259  000101  %********************************************************************
0260  000101  % MULTIPLY SINGLE PRECISION
0261  000101  %********************************************************************
0262  000101  MPY:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0263  000101            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0264  000101            MREFL;
0265  000102            B,R7 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B  % SAVE GPR (NEXT INSTR)
0266  000102            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
0267  000103            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q  % READ STATUS
0268  000103            IDBS,STS T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
0269  000104            B,STS ALUF,MASKDQ ALUD,B  % RESET DYN. OVERFLOW
0270  000104            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0271  000104            20;
0272  000105            A,A B,R2 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B  % COPY A-REG.
0273  000105            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0274  000105            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
0275  000106            B,R3 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B  % READ MEM. OPERAND
0276  000106            IDBS,DBR COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0277  000106            MPY1 CONDENABL;
0278  000107            B,R2 ALUF,-B ALUD,B  % NEGATIVE A-REG.
0279  000107            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0280  000107            COND,COND F,JMP;
0281  000110  MPY1:     B,16 ALUF,ZERO ALUD,Q  % LOAD LOOP-COUNTER
0282  000110            IDBS,BARG COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0283  000110            MPY2 CONDENABL;
0284  000111            A,R3 ALUF,-A ALUD,NONE  % NEG. MEM.-OPERAND
0285  000111            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
0286  000112  MPY2:     B,R5 ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
0287  000112            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0288  000112            COND,LC=0;
0289  000113            A,R2 B,R5 MIS,ZIN ALUF,A+B ALUM,FMU ALUD,SRD  % MAKE RESULT IN R5
0290  000113            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT CYCL,SLOW
0291  000113            LCOUNT;
0292  000114            B,R5 ALUF,PASSB ALUD,SLD
0293  000114            MIS,APT T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
0294  000115            B,R5 MIS,ZIN ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SRD
0295  000115            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0296  000115            COND,F=0 F,NEXT;
0297  000116            B,R4 ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
0298  000116            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0299  000116            MPY3 CONDENABL;
0300  000117            B,R4 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B  % SET DYN. & STAT. OVF.
0301  000117            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0302  000117            60;
0303  000120  MPY3:     A,STS B,R4 ALUF,ORAB ALUD,NONE STS,LO
0304  000120            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
0305  000121            A,A B,R3 ALUF,XORAB ALUD,NONE  % TEST SIGN
0306  000121            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0307  000121            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
0308  000122            B,A ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B
0309  000122            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0310  000122            MPY4 CONDENABL;
0311  000123            B,A ALUF,-B ALUD,B  % INVERT RESULT
0312  000123            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
0313  000124  MPY4:     A,R7 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE  % FILL GPR WITH NEXT
0314  000124            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,SLOW
0315  000124            USGPR;
0316  000125  %********************************************************************
0317  000125  %********************************************************************
0318  000125  % JUMP INSTRUCTIONS
0319  000125  %********************************************************************
0320  000125  JMP:      ALUF,D-1
0321  000125            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0322  000125            MREFJ;

0326  000127  JAZ:      A,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0327  000127            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP CYCL,SLOW

0329  000130            B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B

0331  000130            CJMP CONDENABL;
0332  000131  %********************************************************************

0781  000332  %********************************************************************
0782  000332  % OWN LEVEL, SAVE REGISTER BLOCK IN REGISTER FILE
0783  000332            ALUD,NONE
0784  000332            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0785  000332            COND,LC=0;
0786  000333            A,PIL B,LC ALUF,PASSB ALUD,NONE
0787  000333            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT CYCL,SLOW;
0788  000334  SRB1:     A,X B,R1 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
0789  000334            IDBS,ALU MIS,APT COMM,WRRQ T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
0790  000335            A,REG B,P ALUD,NONE IDBS,REG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT WRRQ;
0791  000336            A,REG B,X ALUD,NONE IDBS,REG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT WRRQ;
0792  000337            A,REG B,T ALUD,NONE IDBS,REG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT WRRQ;
0793  000340            A,REG B,A ALUD,NONE IDBS,REG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT WRRQ;
0794  000341            A,REG B,D ALUD,NONE IDBS,REG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT WRRQ;
0795  000342            A,REG B,L ALUD,NONE IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
0796  000343            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
0797  000343            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0798  000343            377;

0800  000344            IDBS,REG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0801  000344            WRRQ;
0802  000345            ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT WRRQ;
0803  000346            A,REG B,B ALUD,NONE IDBS,REG T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT STEND;
0804  000347  %********************************************************************
0805  000347  % SUBROUTINE TO CHECK FOR OWN LEVEL IN SRB, LRB, IRW, IRR
0806  000347  RBLOK:    ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
0807  000347            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
0808  000350  %********************************************************************
0809  000350  % ENTRY USED BY MOPC IN REGISTER DEPOSIT
0810  000350  OPCRB:    ALUD,NONE
0811  000350            IDBS,DBR COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
0812  000351  %********************************************************************
0813  000351  % ENTRY USED BY IRR AND IRW
0814  000351  IRWRD:    A,PIL ALUF,XORDQ ALUD,Q
0815  000351            IDBS,AARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0816  000351            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
0817  000352            ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,Q
0818  000352            IDBS,ARG T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,SLOW
0819  000352            170;
0820  000353  %********************************************************************
0821  000353  %********************************************************************
0822  000353  % LOAD REGISTER BLOCK
0823  000353  %********************************************************************
0824  000353  LRB:      B,7 ALUD,NONE
0825  000353            IDBS,BARG COMM,LDLC T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0826  000353            RBLOK;
0827  000354            A,P B,R2 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
0828  000354            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
0829  000355            A,X B,R1 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
0830  000355            IDBS,ALU MIS,APT COMM,RDRQ T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
0831  000356            A,REG B,P ALUD,NONE IDBS,DBR COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT RDRQ;
0832  000357            A,REG B,X ALUD,NONE IDBS,DBR COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT RDRQ;
0833  000360            A,REG B,T ALUD,NONE IDBS,DBR COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT RDRQ;
0834  000361            A,REG B,A ALUD,NONE IDBS,DBR COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT RDRQ;
0835  000362            A,REG B,D ALUD,NONE IDBS,DBR COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT RDRQ;
0836  000363            A,REG B,L ALUD,NONE IDBS,DBR COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT RDRQ;
0837  000364            A,REG B,Z ALUD,NONE IDBS,DBR COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT RDRQ;
0838  000365            A,REG B,B ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
0839  000365            IDBS,DBR COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0840  000365            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,POP;
0841  000366            ALUD,NONE
0842  000366            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0843  000366            USGPR CONDENABL;
0844  000367  %********************************************************************
0845  000367  % OWN LEVEL, UNSAVE REGISTER BLOCK
0846  000367            ALUD,NONE
0847  000367            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
0848  000367            COND,LC=0;
0849  000370            A,PIL B,LC ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
0850  000370            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT CYCL,SLOW;
0851  000371            A,R2 B,P ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B  % P IS UNCHANGED
0852  000371            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;

0854  000372            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,SLOW
0855  000372            USGPR;
0856  000373  %********************************************************************
0857  000373  %********************************************************************

0870  000376            A,Z B,Z ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,B

0880  000400            377;
0881  000401            A,REG B,Z ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE

0917  000413            IRW2;

0919  000414            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0920  000414            377 CONDENABL;
0921  000415  %********************************************************************
0922  000415  % STS MUST BE LOADED
0923  000415            A,REG B,Z ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
0924  000415            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,RETURN T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
0925  000416  %********************************************************************
0926  000416  % SUBROUTINE TO CHECK FOR STS AS DESTINATION
0927  000416  IRW2:     B,DEST ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE
0928  000416            IDBS,BARG T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,SLOW
0929  000416            COND,F=0 F,RETURN F,HOLD;
0930  000417  %********************************************************************
0931  000417  %********************************************************************
0932  000417  % LOAD BYTE
0933  000417  %********************************************************************
0934  000417  LBYT:     A,X B,R1 MIS,ZIN ALUF,PASSA ALUM,MIC ALUD,SRB
0935  000417            IDBS,DBR COMM,LDGPR T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
0936  000417            LBYTM;
0937  000420            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
0938  000420            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
0939  000420            LBYTU CONDENABL;

0941  000421  % CODE FOR BOTH UPPER AND LOWER BYTE
0942  000421            B,A ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,B
0943  000421            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0944  000421            377;
0945  000422            ALUD,NONE
0946  000422            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,SLOW
0947  000422            USGPR;
0948  000423  %********************************************************************
0949  000423  % UPPER BYTE
0950  000423  LBYTU:    ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
0951  000423            IDBS,SWAP T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,SLOW;
0952  000424  %********************************************************************
0953  000424  % SUBROUTINE TO CALCULATE ADDRESS IN LBYT AND SBYT
0954  000424  LBYTM:    A,T B,R1 ALUF,A+B ALUD,B
0955  000424            IDBS,ALU MIS,APT COMM,RDRQ T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
0956  000425            A,X B,1 ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,NONE
0957  000425            IDBS,BARG T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,SLOW
0958  000425            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
0959  000426  %********************************************************************
0960  000426  %********************************************************************
0961  000426  % STORE BYTE
0962  000426  %********************************************************************
0963  000426  SBYT:     B,R3 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
0964  000426            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0965  000426            377;
0966  000427            A,A B,R3 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,B  % ISOLATE BYTE
0967  000427            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;

0969  000430            IDBS,DBR COMM,LDGPR T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
0970  000430            LBYTM;

0972  000431            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0973  000431            SBYTU CONDENABL;
0974  000432  %********************************************************************
0975  000432  % LOWER BYTE
0976  000432            B,R2 ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,B
0977  000432            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0978  000432            177400;
0979  000433  %********************************************************************
0980  000433  % BOTH BYTES
0981  000433  SBYT1:    A,R1 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0982  000433            IDBS,ALU MIS,APT COMM,WRRQ T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
0983  000434            A,R3 B,R2 ALUF,ORAB ALUD,NONE
0984  000434            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0985  000434            STEND;

0990  000435            377;

0994  000437            IDBS,SWAP T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0995  000437            SBYT1;

0999  000440  %********************************************************************

1003  000441            B,DEST ALUF,PASSB ALUD,Q

1005  000441            RMPY1 CONDENABL;

1024  000450            RMPY3 CONDENABL;

1026  000451            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,SLOW
1027  000451            USDBR;

1883  001117            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1884  001117            COND,OPCTT1 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
1885  001120  %********************************************************************
1886  001120  % CHECK IF DEVICE NUMBERS 30X IS ON THE CPU-BOARD
1887  001120            ALUF,XORDQ ALUD,NONE
1888  001120            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1889  001120            300;
1890  001121            A,R1 B,3 ALUF,MASKDA ALUD,Q
1891  001121            IDBS,BARG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1892  001121            TERMX CONDENABL;
1893  001122  %********************************************************************
1894  001122  % CHECK IF DEVICE IS IN THE RANGE 10-13
1895  001122            B,10 ALUF,XORDQ ALUD,NONE
1896  001122            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1897  001122            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
1898  001123            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
1899  001123            IDBS,IOR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1900  001123            CLKX CONDENABL;
1901  001124  %********************************************************************
1902  001124  % DEVICE IS NOT ON CPU-BOARD. ISSUE GENERAL IOX
1903  001124            A,R1 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE  % ADDRESS -> IOX
1904  001124            COMM,IOX T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
1905  001125            A,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE  % OUTPUT DATA
1906  001125            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
1907  001126            B,A ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B  % INPUT DATA
1908  001126            IDBS,DBR T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,WAIT;
1909  001127  %********************************************************************
1910  001127  % DEV.NO. 30X IS ON CPU BOARD. JUMP TO VECTOR
1911  001127  TERMX:    AB,STATUS ALUF,PASSD
1912  001127            IDBS,REG T,JMP0-3 T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1913  001127            TRMVC;
1914  001130  %********************************************************************
1915  001130  % DEV.NO. IS 10-13, JUMP TO VECTOR
1916  001130  CLKX:     AB,STATUS ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
1917  001130            IDBS,REG T,JMP0-3 T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
1918  001130            CLKVC;

1920  001131  % SUBROUTINE TO WRITE STATUS-SCRATCH WORD, AND TO WRITE SIOC-REGISTER

1922  001131            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;

1924  001132            IDBS,ALU COMM,SIOC T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,SLOW;

2675  001470            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2676  001470            CONDENABL COND,F11;
2677  001471  %********************************************************************
2678  001471  % CPU IS IN ION
2679  001471            ALUF,PASSD
2680  001471            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
2681  001471            100013;
2682  001472  %********************************************************************
2683  001472  % LOOP TO FIND HIGHEST ENABLED LEVEL
2684  001472            B,R3 ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SLD
2685  001472            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT CYCL,SLOW;
2686  001473            B,LC ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q  % NEW LEVEL -> Q
2687  001473            IDBS,BARG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2688  001473            PLINT;
2689  001474  %********************************************************************
2690  001474  % THE WAIT-INSTRUCTION WAS ON LEVEL 14
2691  001474  WT14:     ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
2692  001474            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2693  001474            40000;
2694  001475            PIC,MCLPID ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
2695  001475            IDBS,ALU COMM,EPIC T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2696  001475            CHKIT;
2697  001476  %********************************************************************
2698  001476  %********************************************************************
2699  001476  % HARDWARE INTERRUPT HANDLING (EXT. OR INT. INTERRUPTS)
2700  001476  % ENTERED FROM INTERRUPT VECTOR (3740-3757)
2701  001476  %********************************************************************
2702  001476  % INTERNAL INTERRUPTS
2703  001476  EXT14:    B,P ALUF,B-1 ALUD,B
2704  001476            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2705  001476            27;
2706  001477            A,16 ALUD,NONE
2707  001477            IDBS,BMG COMM,SMPID T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2708  001500  %********************************************************************
2709  001500  % ONLY LEVEL 15 IS HIGHER THAN STATUS 27

2711  001500            IDBS,GPR COMM,EPIC T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2712  001500            PLINT;
2713  001501  %********************************************************************
2714  001501  % HARDWARE INTERRUPT EXCEPT LEVEL 14
2715  001501  XINT:     B,P ALUF,B-1 ALUD,B
2716  001501            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2717  001502  %********************************************************************
2718  001502  % LEVEL IN Q SHOULD BE ENTERED

2720  001502            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2721  001502            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
2722  001503            B,R6 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
2723  001503            IDBS,SWAP T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2724  001503            PLV0 CONDENABL;
2725  001504  %********************************************************************
2726  001504  % NEW LEVEL IS NOT LEVEL 0, UPDATE PID
2727  001504            A,LC ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
2728  001504            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2729  001505            AB,PID ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,B
2730  001505            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2731  001506            AB,PID ALUF,PASSB ALUD,NONE
2732  001506            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2733  001507  PLV0:     A,PIL ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
2734  001507            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2735  001510            A,LC ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,NONE
2736  001510            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2737  001510            COND,F=0 F,RETURN F,HOLD;
2738  001511            ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,NONE
2739  001511            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2740  001511            140000 CONDENABL;
2741  001512  %********************************************************************
2742  001512  % NEW LEVEL IS UNEQUAL TO OLD LEVEL
2743  001512            A,LC ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
2744  001512            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2745  001512            COND,COND F,JMP F,HOLD;
2746  001513            A,PIL B,R5 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
2747  001513            IDBS,AARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2748  001513            LVSWP CONDENABL;
2749  001514  %********************************************************************
2750  001514  % OLD LEVEL IS NOT 14 OR 15
2751  001514            AB,PVL ALUF,PASSB ALUD,NONE
2752  001514            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2753  001515  %********************************************************************
2754  001515  % CHANGE LEVEL ROUTINE. Q = NEW LEVEL
2755  001515  LVSWP:    AB,ACTLV ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,B
2756  001515            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2757  001516            ALUD,NONE
2758  001516            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2759  001516            7;
2760  001517            B,Z ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
2761  001517            IDBS,STS T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
2762  001517            COND,LC=0;
2763  001520            A,PIL B,LC ALUF,PASSB ALUD,Q  % SAVE LOOP
2764  001520            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT CYCL,SLOW;
2765  001521            B,Z ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,B
2766  001521            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
2767  001521            170000;

2769  001522            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDPIL T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
2770  001523            ALUD,NONE
2771  001523            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2772  001523            7;
2773  001524            AB,ACTLV ALUF,PASSB ALUD,NONE
2774  001524            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW;
2775  001525            A,PIL B,LC ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B  % UNSAVE LOOP
2776  001525            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT CYCL,SLOW;

2778  001526            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
2779  001526            INIT;

2783  001527            37760;

3069  001657            ALUF,D-Q ALUD,Q  % CHECK PID
3070  001657            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,POP CYCL,SLOW
3071  001657            176000;
3072  001660            ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
3073  001660            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3074  001660            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;

3076  001661  % STERR IF PID NOT 176000 (6400)
3077  001661            A,17 B,STS ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3078  001661            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3079  001661            STERR CONDENABL;

3089  001664            IDBS,ARG T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,SLOW
3090  001664            177777;

3095  001665            400;

3100  001666            400;

3104  001667            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3105  001667            DYTP2;

3107  001670            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDPIL T,RETURN T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;

3111  001671            IDBS,ARG COMM,SIOC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3112  001671            30140;
3113  001672            AB,STATUS ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
3114  001672            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3115  001673            AB,MANIR ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
3116  001673            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3117  001674            AB,TXT1 ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
3118  001674            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3119  001675            AB,TXT2 ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
3120  001675            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3121  001676            AB,SINGL ALUF,ZERO ALUD,Q
3122  001676            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3123  001676            LVSWP;

3129  001700            AB,SCRAM ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3130  001701            AB,OCTNR ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3131  001702            AB,CURNR ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;

3133  001704            AB,OCTN2 ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3134  001705            AB,DEPOS ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,SLOW;
3135  001706  %********************************************************************

3140  001706            IDBS,ARG COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3141  001706            443;
3142  001707  %********************************************************************

3144  001707  % FROM INTERRUPT VECTOR IF THE CPU IS IN STOP (3760 OR 3770)
3145  001707  %********************************************************************

3147  001707            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3148  001707            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3149  001710            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3150  001710            IDBS,IOR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3151  001710            STOP2 CONDENABL;
3152  001711  %********************************************************************
3153  001711  % STOP FOLLOWS DIRECTLY AFTER MASTER CLEAR. STAND-BY IS NOT OK
3154  001711            A,R5 ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,NONE
3155  001711            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3156  001711            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;

3159  001712            STOP2 CONDENABL;
3160  001713  %********************************************************************
3161  001713  % PANEL IS LOCKED WHEN POWER-UP, OR LOAD IS PRESSED
3162  001713  ALDLO:    ALUD,NONE  % USE ALD-SWITCH
3163  001713            IDBS,ALU T,JMPALD T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3164  001713            ALDVC;
3165  001714  %********************************************************************

3177  001716            ETLO1 CONDENABL;

3183  001717            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;

3188  001721            A,D B,R1 ALUF,A-1 ALUD,B
3189  001721            T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3190  001721            IOXG;

3200  001723            IOXG;
3201  001724  %********************************************************************

3215  001727            IOXG;

3217  001730  % IOX N+4 READ STATUS
3218  001730  MAS2:     A,D B,R1 ALUF,D+A ALUD,B
3219  001730            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3220  001730            IOXG;

3236  001735            B,P ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B

3238  001735            ESCAP;
3239  001736  %********************************************************************
3240  001736  %********************************************************************
3241  001736  % RESTART, ENTERED FROM INTERRUPT VECTOR (3766)
3242  001736  % ONLY IF STAND-BY POWER IS OK, OR RESTART BUTTON PRESSED

3245  001736            IDBS,IOR COMM,CLRTC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3246  001736            COND,STP F,RETURN F,POP;
3247  001737            A,15 ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,Q
3248  001737            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3249  001737            CONDENABL COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
3250  001740  %********************************************************************
3251  001740  % ONLY IF IN STOP MODE
3252  001740            AB,MACL ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE
3253  001740            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3254  001740            RSTR1 CONDENABL;
3255  001741  %********************************************************************
3256  001741  % IF LOCKED
3257  001741            AB,MACL ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
3258  001741            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,JMPALD T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3259  001741            ALDVC;
3260  001742            ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
3261  001742            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3262  001742            COND,F15 F,NEXT;
3263  001743  %********************************************************************
3264  001743  % LOAD IF BIT 15=1, START IN ADDRESS 20 IF BIT 15=0
3265  001743            ALUD,NONE

3267  001743            LOAD1 CONDENABL;
3268  001744  %********************************************************************
3269  001744  % START CPU IN ADDRESS 20
3270  001744  RSTR2:    A,2 B,P ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
3271  001744            IDBS,AARG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3272  001744            CONT;
3273  001745  %********************************************************************
3274  001745  % IF UNLOCKED, DO NOTHING JUST AFTER MASTER CLEAR, ELSE START IN 20
3275  001745  RSTR1:    AB,MACL ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
3276  001745            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3277  001745            INIT CONDENABL;
3278  001746            ALUD,NONE
3279  001746            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3280  001746            RSTR2;
3281  001747  %********************************************************************
3282  001747  %********************************************************************
3283  001747  % LOAD BUTTON IS PRESSED, ENTERED FROM INTERRUPT VECTOR (3764 OR 3774)

3286  001747            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW

3288  001750            ALUD,NONE

3291  001751  %********************************************************************
3292  001751  %********************************************************************
3293  001751  % CPU IS NOT IN STOP WHEN LOAD IS PRESSED, OR
3294  001751  % CONTINUE BUTTON IS PRESSED, ENTERED FROM INTERRUPT VECTOR (3765 OR 3775)
3295  001751  %********************************************************************

3297  001751            IDBS,ALU COMM,START T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3298  001751            ESCAP;
3299  001752  %********************************************************************
3300  001752  %********************************************************************
3301  001752  % $ OR & IS TYPED TO MOPC

3305  001752            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW

3308  001753  % USE ALD-SWITCH IF NO OCTAL NUMBER GIVEN
3309  001753            ALUD,NONE
3310  001753            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3311  001753            ALDLO CONDENABL;
3312  001754            ALUD,NONE
3313  001754            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3314  001754            LOAD1;
3315  001755  %********************************************************************
3316  001755  %********************************************************************
3317  001755  % BINARY LOADER. LOAD CODE BIT 13 IS 0.
3318  001755  %********************************************************************
3319  001755  ETLO1:    B,D ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B  % DEV.NO. -> D
3320  001755            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3321  001755            DVACT;

3323  001756            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;

3325  001757            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3326  001757            ASS8;
3327  001760            A,R2 B,15 ALUF,A-D ALUD,NONE

3332  001761            SEEK CONDENABL;

3334  001762  % LAST CHAR. IS NOT CR
3335  001762            ALUD,NONE

3337  001762            EXFOU CONDENABL;

3342  001763            SIKI;

3344  001764  % EXCLAMATION MARK (!) FOUND
3345  001764  EXFOU:    A,STS B,STS ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
3346  001764            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3347  001765            B,L ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B  % READ CORE ADDR.
3348  001765            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3349  001765            BIN;

3351  001766            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW

3353  001767            A,Z B,T ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
3354  001767            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3355  001770  %********************************************************************
3356  001770  % LOOP TO STORE PROGRAM IN MEMORY
3357  001770  STLP:     ALUD,NONE  % READ ONE WORD
3358  001770            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
3359  001770            BIN;
3360  001771            B,T ALUF,B-1 ALUD,B  % DECR. WORD COUNT
3361  001771            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3362  001771            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
3363  001772            A,X B,X ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B,YA  % INCR. CORE ADDR.
3364  001772            IDBS,ALU MIS,PT COMM,WRRQ T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3365  001772            COND,COND F,JMP F,HOLD;
3366  001773            A,Z B,L ALUF,A+B ALUD,B,YA  % ACCUMULATE CH.SM.
3367  001773            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
3368  001773            STLP CONDENABL;
3369  001774  %********************************************************************
3370  001774  % ALL WORDS ARE PLACED IN MEMORY
3371  001774            ALUD,NONE  % READ CHECKSUM
3372  001774            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
3373  001774            BIN;
3374  001775            A,Z B,L ALUF,XORAB ALUD,NONE  % TEST CH.SM.
3375  001775            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3376  001775            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
3377  001776            ALUD,NONE
3378  001776            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3379  001776            ILLEG CONDENABL;

3381  001777  % CHECKSUM OK, READ ACTION CODE

3383  001777            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,SLOW
3384  001777            ASS8;
3385  002000            A,R2 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE

3387  002000            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;

3450  002022  %********************************************************************
3451  002022  % SUBROUTINE USED BY BINARY LOADER TO READ TWO BYTES AS ONE 16-BIT WORD
3452  002022  BIN:      ALUD,NONE  % READ BYTE 1

3454  002022            INCH;
